Maybe use SOCP relaxation: For each k, C_k = a^T T_k a. Since T_k indefinite, but on nonnegative orthant maybe can bound by something convex? We could relax a_i a_j ≤? Use AM-GM: a_i a_j ≤ (λ a_i^2 + a_j^2/λ)/2. Convex upper bounds, not lower. To enforce C_k <= t, need upper bound on sum products. Since a_i a_j is convex over nonnegative? Actually product of nonnegative variables is not convex (Hessian [[0,1/2],[1/2,0]] indefinite). But using log variables: a_i=e^{x_i}; product=e^{x_i+x_j}, convex in x. Objective max of sums of exponentials is convex in x! If we fix sum? Wait a_i=e^{x_i}, C_k=sum e^{x_i+x_j}; log-sum-exp of linear forms is convex. Log-softmax L-BFGS:
- Global variables `_LSZ_N`, `_LSZ_BETA`, `_LSZ_LAST`.
- Function `lsz_objective(z)`:
   - n=_LSZ_N; z includes fixed last 0? length n-1.
   - full_z = concatenate(z, [0]); maxz=full_z.max(); a=exp(full_z-maxz); a/=a.sum().
   - C=convolve_self(a); d=C*(2n-1) (sum=1); m=max(d); w=exp(_LSZ_BETA*(d-m)); s=w/w.sum(); soft=m+(1/_LSZ_BETA)*log(s.sum()) (or log(sum exp)? stable). Actually logsumexp stable: m + (1/β)log(sumw). Objective=soft. Return (soft, grad).
   - Weights s normalized. H=weighted_cross(a, s) (where H_l=∑s_k a_{k-l}); G=2H. b=dot(a,G). grad_z = a*(G-b). grad_v=grad_z[:-1]. Return float(soft), grad_v.
- Use `minimize(lsz_objective, z0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': maybe 200, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'disp':False})`.

Implement FFT cross-correlation:
```
def weighted_cross(a, s):
    n = len(a); L = 2*n-1
    # s length L
    s_rev = s[::-1]
    N = 1 << (len(a)+L-2).bit_length()? Need conv length n+L-1 = 3n-2.
    N = 1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fs = np.fft.rfft(s_rev, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fs, N)[:L]  # indices 0..L-1?
    # H_i = conv[L-1-i] for i=0..n-1? Wait conv length n+L-1, but we only need first L? Let's test indices: m=L-1-i ranges from L-1 down to 0. These are within conv length, yes. So conv[:L][L-1::-1]
    return conv[:L][::-1][:n]
```
Check with direct: n=3, L=5, a length3, s_rev length5, conv length7. H0 = ∑_k s0? k? formula H_i=∑_k s_k a_{k-i} if valid (j=k-i). For i=0: ∑_k s_k a_k (k=0..2). conv at m=L-1=4: (a*s_rev)[4]=∑_j a_j s_rev[4-j]. s_rev[4-j]=s[L-1-(4-j)]=s[4-(4-j)]=s[j]. valid j0..2 => ∑a_j s_j. Good. H1 = conv[3]: ∑_j a_j s_rev[3-j]=s[3? L-1=4, 4-(3-j)=1+j] => s_{1+j}, j such that 1+j<5, but j up to2 gives s1,s2,s3; a3 invalid; valid s_k with k-i=1+j in0..n-1 => k=i+j, yes. Good. So H = conv[:L][L-1::-1]. The length n slice. 

Convolution self `convolve_self(a)`:
```
L=2n-1; N=1 << (L-1).bit_length(); fa=rfft(a,N); c=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]; maximum(c,0)
```

Optimization custom details:
```
def smooth_local_search(a, budget_s, seed, beta_start=5, beta_end=200):
  a = normalize_mean(a, n)
  best = a.copy(); best_score = fast_score(a)
  rng = default_rng(seed)
  eta=0.5; max_eta=3; min_eta=0.01
  it=0; stagnant=0
  deadline = t0+budget
  C=conv_self(a)
  while deadline:
    # beta schedule: use it total? we know budget but not iterations. Use fraction based on time? initial. beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*min(1, (time- t0)/budget)
    x = C * (2*n-1)/(a.sum()**2)  # should sum avg ~1? Since C sum=S^2, x sum=2n-1, mean~1. Max target.
    m=x.max(); w=np.exp(beta*(x-m)); w/=w.sum()
    H=weighted_cross(a,w)
    g=H-H.mean()
    scale=max(abs(g)); if scale<1e-14 break/stagnant
    g/=scale
    logf=-eta*g; clip(-3,3)
    cand=a*np.exp(logf); normalize_mean
    cand_score=fast_score(cand)
    if finite:
       if cand_score<best_score: best=cand; best_score=...
       if cand_score <= score*(1+1e-6): a=cand; C=conv_self(cand); score=cand_score; eta=min(max_eta,eta*1.05?) ...
       else: eta=max(min_eta,eta*0.7); stagnant++
    if stagnant>... perturb
    it++
  return best, best_score
```
Need score variable updated current. For backtracking, if accepted with worse than best but better than current? Use previous `score` current. If not accept, revert? We only update a on accept; cand not. If rejected, keep a,C. Stagnant reset on best improvement maybe.

Eta scale: g normalized to max1. Multiplicative factor exp(-eta*g) with eta~0.5 gives 40% changes. Could be too large; but backtracking. For large n, small changes maybe. Use eta=0.25.

Normalization: `normalize_mean(a,n)` sets sum=n. If some zeros, exp keeps positive? If a has zeros, logf * a zero remains zero; good. But if a positive, stay positive. If a has zeros due clipping, stuck. Ensure starts positive epsilon.

Objective numeric stability:
```
def _lsz_func(z):
   n = _LSZ_N; beta = _LSZ_BETA
   full = np.concatenate((z, np.zeros(1)))  # But each call allocate.
   full[-1] = 0
   mz = full.max()
   a = np.exp(full - mz)
   a /= a.sum()
   C = conv_self(a)
   d = C * float(2*n-1)  # since sum1
   m = d.max()
   w = np.exp(beta*(d-m))
   wsum = w.sum()
   val = m + math.log(wsum)/beta
   wh = w/wsum
   H = weighted_cross(a, wh)
   G = 2*H
   b = np.dot(a, G)
   grad = a*(G - b)
   return val, grad[:-1]
```

Pseudo:
```
def projected_multiplicative_update(a, eta, beta, topk=0, K=8):
  C=conv_self(a)
  S=sum(a)
  d=C*(2n-1)/S^2  # mean1
  if topk>0:
    idx=argpartition(d,-K)[-K:]; w=zeros; w[idx]=1/K
  else:
    m=d.max(); w=exp(beta*(d-m)); /sum
  H=weighted_cross(a,w)
  g=H-H.mean(); g/=max(abs(g)+eps)
  cand=a*exp(-eta*g); normalize_mean
  return cand, score
```

Implementation detail for weights:
```
def weights_from_d(d, beta, K):
   if K>0:
      K=min(K,len(d)); idx=np.argpartition(d, -K)[-K:] if K< len(d) else np.arange(len(d))
      w=np.zeros_like(d); w[idx]=1.0/K; return w
   else:
      m=d.max(); w=np.exp(beta*(d-m)); s=w.sum(); if s==0: w at m; else w/=s
```

cand = normalize_mean(cand)
```

L-BFGS refinement function:
```
def refine_lbfgs(a, budget, seed, beta_seq=(20,50,100,300)):
   for beta in beta_seq:
      if time>budget break
      set globals
      z0 = to_z(a)
      res = minimize(_lsz_func, z0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options=..., callback=_lsz_callback)
      a_new = z_to_a(res.x)
      sc=fast_score(a_new)
      if sc<best: a=a_new
   return a,best
```
Callback checks time. Need global `_LSZ_STOP`. The callback returns True to stop. But if callback stops due time, res.x available.

If s>eps, a_i_new = c * a_i/s; a_rev_new=c - a_i_new. If s<=eps, set both c/2. Then normalize? Pair sum c, total n*c/2. If c=2, total n. If want mean1. Good. - `upsample_to(seq, n)`: linear interp, then maybe normalize.

- `sine_anti(coeffs, n)`: t=arange/(n-1); val=1.0; for m,c in enumerate: val += c*np.sin(2π*(m+1)*t); clip positive; then antisym_seq(val,2). This enforces sum2 and may alter coefficients but okay.
- `random_sine_anti(rng,n,max_modes=8,max_amp=0.8)`: coefficients amplitudes random decaying: amp_m = max_amp * rng.uniform(0.2,1)/(m+1)^0.7, sign. Use.

Backtracking:
```
score=fast_score_from_conv(a,C)
best=...
eta=0.35; noise=0.01; stagnant=0; no_accept=0; it=0
while time < deadline:
  frac=(time-t0)/budget if budget>0 else 1
  if frac<0.6: beta=beta_start + (beta_end*0.3 - beta_start)*frac/0.6; K=0
  elif frac<0.85: beta=beta_end*0.3 + (beta_end - beta_end*0.3)*(frac-0.6)/0.25; K=0
  else: beta=0; K=cycle[it%len]
  w=weights
  H=weighted_cross(a,w)
  g=H-H.mean(); sc=maxabs; if sc<1e-14: if stagnant... break
  g/=sc
  if noise>0: g += rng.normal(0, noise, n)
  logf = -eta*g; clip(-2,2)
  cand = a*np.exp(logf); normalize_mean(cand)
  C2=convolve_self(cand); cand_score=...
  if finite:
    if cand_score < best: best=...
    if cand_score <= score*(1+1e-9):
       old=score; a=cand; C=C2; score=cand_score; stagnant=0
       if cand_score < old*(1-1e-10): eta=min(1.2, eta*1.08); noise=max(0.002, noise*0.99)
       else: eta=max(0.02, eta*0.95)
    else:
       eta=max(0.005, eta*0.7); no_accept+=1; stagnant+=1
  else: ...
  if no_accept>100: eta=0.5; noise=0.1; no_accept=0; stagnant=min(stagnant,10)
  if stagnant>300:
     # perturb from best
     a=normalize_mean(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.3, n)))
     C=convolve_self(a); score=fast; if score>best*1.2: a=best.copy(); score=best_score
     eta=0.6; noise=0.05; stagnant=0; no_accept=0
  it+=1
return best,best_score
```
Stagnant increments if no best improvement; if accept but no improvement maybe small. We can track `last_best_it`. If it-last_best_it>500 perturb.

Stable softmax:
```
full = np.empty(n); full[:-1]=z; full[-1]=0
mz=np.max(full)
ex=np.exp(full-mz); a=ex/ex.sum()
C=convolve_self(a)
d=C*float(2*n-1)
m=np.max(d)
if _LSZ_BETA>0:
  w=np.exp(_LSZ_BETA*(d-m)); s=np.sum(w)
  if s<=0 or not finite: val=m; wh=np.zeros_like(d); wh.argmax=1
  else: val=m+np.log(s)/_LSZ_BETA; wh=w/s
else: wh top1
H=weighted_cross(a, wh)
G=2*H; b=np.dot(a,G); grad=a*(G-b)
# scale objective to C scale: invL=1/float(2*n-1)
return val*invL, grad*invL
```
If beta extremely large and d differences >20, weights for nonmax underflow; okay, wh top max. If multiple max equal, log(s) handles.

`convolve_self(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
n=a.size
if n==0: return np.empty(0)
L=2*n-1
N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
c=np.maximum(c,0)
return c
```

Let's write `local_search(a,budget_s,seed,beta_start=3,beta_end=80,topk_values=(1,2,4,8,16,32))`:
Use `t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s; rng=...`
Initial a normalized target n.
C=conv_self; score=...
best=a.copy(); best_score=score
eta=0.35; max_eta=1.5; min_eta=0.005; noise=0.02; last_best=0; stagn=0; noacc=0; it=0
While time<deadline-0.1:
 if budget_s>0: frac=min(1,(time-t0)/budget_s) else frac=1
 # beta/K
 if frac<0.55:
   beta=beta_start + (max(20,beta_end*0.25)-beta_start)*(frac/0.55)
   K=0
 elif frac<0.85:
   beta=beta_end*0.25 + (beta_end- beta_end*0.25)*((frac-0.55)/0.30)
   K=0
 else:
   beta=0.0
   K=topk_values[it % len(topk_values)]
 d=C * float(2*n-1) / (S*S) ? We ensure S=n; but use float(S). Since after normalize S target n. `scale = float(2*n-1) / (S*S)`. d=C*scale. avg ~1.
 w = weights_from_d(d,beta,K)
 H = weighted_cross(a,w)
 g=H-H.mean()
 sc= np.max(np.abs(g))
 if not finite or sc<1e-14:
   # no gradient; perturb or break
   if it-last_best>200: perturb else break
 else:
   g/=sc
   if noise>1e-6: g += rng.normal(0,noise,n)
   logf=-eta*g; logf=np.clip(logf,-2.0,2.0)
   cand=a*np.exp(logf)
   cand=normalize_mean(cand,n)
   C2=convolve_self(cand); S2=cand.sum(); cand_score=...
   if finite:
      if cand_score < best_score: best=cand.copy(); best_score=cand_score; last_best=it
      if cand_score <= score + 1e-12:
         old=score; a=cand; C=C2; S=S2; score=cand_score; noacc=0
         if score < old*(1-1e-10): eta=min(max_eta,eta*1.08); noise=max(0.002,noise*0.99)
         else: eta=max(min_eta,eta*0.95)
      else:
         eta=max(min_eta,eta*0.7); noacc+=1
   else:
      eta=max(min_eta,eta*0.7); noacc+=1
   if noacc>80:
      eta=0.6; noise=0.08; noacc=0; stagn=min(stagn,50)
   if it-last_best > 600:
      # perturb
      gain=0.25 if it-last_best<1500 else 0.5
      pert=best.copy()*np.exp(rng.normal(0,gain,n))
      pert=normalize_mean(pert,n)
      if fast_score(pert) > best_score*1.25: pert=best.copy()
      a=pert; C=conv_self(a); score=fast_score(a)
      eta=0.6; noise=0.05; last_best=it? Should not reset because no improvement. Set it=last_best+601? We can just set `it = last_best`? No. Set停滞 counter.
      noacc=0
 it+=1
return best,best_score
```

`antisym_sequence` implementation details:
```
def antisym_sequence(x, c=2.0):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel(); n=x.size
    if n==0: return x
    a=np.empty(n)
    if n%2==1:
       m=n//2
       left=x[:m]; right=x[n-1:m-1:-1]
       # handle middle x[m]
    else: m=n//2
    s=left+right
    idx_all=np.arange(m)
    good=s>1e-12
    a[idx_all[good]] = c * left[good] / s[good]
    a[n-1-idx_all[good]] = c - a[idx_all[good]]  # cannot index fancy with expression? Need assign in loop?
```
Fancy assignment to two indices: We can create arrays:
```
li=idx_all[good]
new_left = c*left[li]/s[li]
a[li]=new_left
a[n-1-li] = c - new_left
bad=~good
if np.any(bad): a[li_bad]=c/2; a[n-1-li_bad]=c/2
```
For odd, middle: We can set a[m]=c/2 + maybe x[m]? But for even n not needed. If parent odd, upsample to even. Still implement:
```
if n%2==1:
  m=n//2; middle=x[m]
  # after pairs, a[m]=middle if middle finite and 0<=middle<=c else c/2; then normalize? Pair sum not total. We'll not use odd.
```
Return a.

def optimize_length(n,budget_s,parent,seed,out_pool):
    if budget_s < 10: return one
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts=generate_starts(n,parent,rng)
    # fourier DE if budget >600
    if budget_s > 600:
       fb=min(180, 0.15*budget_s)
       seq,sc=fourier_anti_search(n,fb,seed+999)
       if seq is not None: add to starts and out_pool
    starts.sort(key=pair_score)
    # quick local
    if time < deadline - 60:
       qt=min(240,0.18*budget_s)
       k=min(10,len(starts))
       for i in range(k):
          if time >= deadline - 60: break
          b=qt/k; if b<3: b=3
          seq,sc=local_search(starts[i][1], b, seed+i, beta_start=2, beta_end=30)
          add_candidate_pool(out_pool,seq,sc)
    # long local
    out_pool.sort; select top 6
    lt=min(0.42*budget_s, deadline-time-min(300,0.35*budget_s))? Need reserve.
```

`_stable_sigmoid`:
```
def _stable_sigmoid(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    out=np.empty(v.shape,float)
    pos=v>=0
    out[pos]=1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-v[pos]))
    ev=np.exp(v[~pos])
    out[~pos]=ev/(1.0+ev)
    return out
```
For scalar? v array. Good.

```
def _add_candidate(pool, seq, score, max_size=40):
    try:
        sc=float(score)
    except: return
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    if seq is None: return
    a=np.asarray(seq,float)
    if a.size==0: return
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-8: return
    a*=a.size/s
    for i,(oldsc,olda) in enumerate(pool):
       if olda.size==a.size and float(np.max(np.abs(a-olda))) < 0.01:
           return  # skip duplicate? Maybe if new score better, update?
    pool.append((sc,a.copy()))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool)>max_size: pool.pop()
```

`normalize_mean` use floor eps:
```
def normalize_mean(a,target):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1e6)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-30:
       return np.full(a.size, target/a.size if a.size>0 else 1.0)
   a = a * (target/s)
   a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
   a = a * (target/a.sum())
   return a
```
If target may be None: default len. For n=0? not.

Now, `fourier_anti_search`:
```
def fourier_anti_search(n,budget,seed):
  global _FOURIER_N, _FOURIER_BASES
  if budget < 30 or n<16: return None,inf
  _FOURIER_N=n
  t=np.arange(n)/(n-1)
  modes=min(6, max(3, int(math.log2(n))))  # for 512 modes 9? maybe 6
  bases=[]
  for m in range(1,modes+1): bases.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t))
  _FOURIER_BASES=bases
  bounds=[]
  amps=[0.95,0.7,0.5,0.35,0.25,0.15]
  for m in range(modes): amp=amps[min(m,len(amps)-1)]; bounds.append((-amp,amp))
  maxiter=min(80, max(20, int(budget/3)))
  try:
     res=scipy.optimize.differential_evolution(_fourier_obj, bounds, seed=seed, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=12, tol=1e-5, polish=False, workers=1, initial='sobol')
  except TypeError: # if initial not supported
     res=...
  except Exception: return None,inf
  if res is None or res.x is None: return None,inf
  val=1.0
  for b,c in zip(bases,res.x): val += c*b
  mn=float(np.min(val))
  if mn < -1e-4:
     # try repair
     val=np.maximum(val,0.0)
     val=antisym_sequence(val,2.0)
  a=normalize_mean(val,n)
  sc=fast_score(a)
  return a,sc
```
`initial='sobol'` may require `qmc`; older scipy may TypeError. Use default to be safe. Remove initial.
`workers=1` accepted. If budget small, maxiter20.

def smooth_perturb(a,rng,gain=0.3):
   a=np.array(a,float).copy(); n=a.size
   if n>2:
     t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
     z=zeros
     for m in 1..3: z += (gain/(0.5+0.5m))*cos(2πm t + phase)
     a*=exp(z)
   if n>8 and rng.random()<0.4: random local band
   return normalize_mean(a,n)
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Main ingredients:
- FFT-based fast objective/gradient evaluation.
- Symmetry-breaking starts, including anti-complementary (a_i + a_{n-1-i} = 2)
  sigmoid/sine/block families.
- A multiplicative projected-gradient refinement with softmax-over-convolution
  coefficients, transitioning to active-peak top-K updates.
- Restricted L-BFGS refinement on the anti-complementary manifold.
- Full L-BFGS refinement with a smooth log-sum-exp criterion.
- Optional longer-length refinement after the base length has been optimized.

The entry point is propose_candidate().
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time
import scipy.optimize

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global state used by top-level objective functions passed to scipy.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_LSZ_N = 0
_LSZ_BETA = 1.0
_LSZ_DEADLINE = 0.0
_LSZ_CALLS = 0
_LSZ_MAXFUN = 500

_ANTI_N = 0
_ANTI_BETA = 1.0
_ANTI_DEADLINE = 0.0
_ANTI_CALLS = 0
_ANTI_MAXFUN = 500

_FOURIER_N = 0
_FOURIER_BASES = None


def _pair_score(pair):
    return float(pair[0])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _stable_sigmoid(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.empty(v.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = v >= 0
    out[pos] = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-v[pos]))
    ev = np.exp(v[~pos])
    out[~pos] = ev / (1.0 + ev)
    return out


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def convolve_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def weighted_cross(a, w):
    """
    H_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}, where valid indices are used.
    Evaluated by one FFT convolution.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != L:
        # Defensive fallback: direct sum, only for tiny sizes.
        H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
            return H
        w = w / ws
        for k in range(L):
            if abs(w[k]) < 1e-16:
                continue
            start = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            stop = min(n, k + 1)
            H[start:stop] += w[k] * a[k - start:k - stop + 1][::-1]
        return H

    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = w / ws

    # Need convolution a * w[::-1], take indices L-1, L-2, ..., L-n.
    out_len = n + L - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N)[:L]
    rev = conv[::-1]
    return rev[:n]


def normalize_mean(a, target=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    if target is None:
        target = float(a.size)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0e6)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-30:
        return np.full(a.size, target / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (target / s)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-30:
        return np.full(a.size, target / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (target / s)


def make_safe(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0e6)


def fast_score_from_conv(a, c):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    cm = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))


def fast_score(a):
    a = make_safe(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = convolve_self(a)
    return fast_score_from_conv(a, c)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Sequence construction / transformation helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def upsample_sequence(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == n:
        return x.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, x.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(xn, xo, x)


def antisym_sequence(x, c=2.0):
    """
    Return y of same length with y_i + y_{n-1-i} = c, preserving the relative
    proportions within each pair as much as possible.
    """
    x = make_safe(x)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        m = n // 2
        left = x[:m]
        right = x[n - 1:m - 1:-1]
        s = left + right
        idx = np.arange(m)
        good = s > 1e-12
        li = idx[good]
        vl = c * left[li] / s[li]
        a[li] = vl
        a[n - 1 - li] = c - vl
        bad = ~good
        if np.any(bad):
            lb = idx[bad]
            a[lb] = 0.5 * c
            a[n - 1 - lb] = 0.5 * c
        mid = x[m]
        a[m] = mid if np.isfinite(mid) and 0.0 <= mid <= c else 0.5 * c
        return a

    m = n // 2
    left = x[:m]
    right = x[n - 1:m - 1:-1]
    s = left + right
    idx = np.arange(m)
    good = s > 1e-12
    li = idx[good]
    vl = c * left[li] / s[li]
    a[li] = vl
    a[n - 1 - li] = c - vl
    if np.any(~good):
        lb = idx[~good]
        a[lb] = 0.5 * c
        a[n - 1 - lb] = 0.5 * c
    return a


def sigmoid_anti(k, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    a = 1.0 + np.tanh(float(k) * (t - 0.5))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return antisym_sequence(a, 2.0)


def sine_anti(coeffs, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    a = np.full(n, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    for m, c in enumerate(coeffs, start=1):
        a += float(c) * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * m * t)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return antisym_sequence(a, 2.0)


def random_block_anti(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 2:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = n // 2
    nb = int(rng.integers(3, max(4, m // 12 + 4)))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, nb)
    y = rng.uniform(0.05, 1.95, nb)
    # Add a small chance of a zero block to create sharp features.
    if rng.random() < 0.15:
        y[int(rng.integers(0, nb))] = 0.05
    u = np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m), x, y)
    u = np.clip(u, 1e-4, 2.0 - 1e-4)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = u
    a[m:] = 2.0 - u[::-1]
    return a


def make_harmonic(n, rng, max_freq=8, max_amp=1.5):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, max_freq + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(-max_amp, max_amp) / ((m + 1.0) ** 0.7)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph)
    amp = rng.uniform(-max_amp, max_amp)
    z += amp * np.cos(math.pi * (2.0 * t - 1.0))
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    a = np.exp(z)
    return normalize_mean(a, n)


def smooth_perturb(a, rng, gain=0.30):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 2:
        return normalize_mean(a, n)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 4):
        amp = gain / (0.5 + 0.5 * m)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph)
    a *= np.exp(z)
    if n > 8 and rng.random() < 0.35:
        width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 200), max(2, n // 10 + 1)))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        stop = min(n, start + width)
        a[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, gain, stop - start))
    return normalize_mean(a, n)


def add_start_list(starts, a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        a = upsample_sequence(a, n) if a.size else np.ones(n)
    a = make_safe(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    a = normalize_mean(a, n)
    sc = fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        sc = fast_score(a)
    starts.append((float(sc), a))


def add_candidate(pool, seq, score, max_size=40):
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    a = make_safe(seq)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-8:
        return
    a = a * (float(a.size) / s)
    # Avoid adding near-duplicates.
    for _, old in pool:
        if old.size == a.size:
            try:
                if float(np.max(np.abs(a - old))) < 0.01:
                    return
            except Exception:
                pass
    pool.append((sc, a.copy()))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool) > max_size:
        pool.pop()


def generate_starts(n, parent, rng):
    n = int(n)
    starts = []

    # Uniform baseline.
    add_start_list(starts, np.ones(n), n)

    # Anti-complementary sigmoid starts.
    for k in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 6.0, 8.0, 12.0, 16.0):
        add_start_list(starts, sigmoid_anti(k, n), n)

    # Random anti-complementary sine starts.
    for _ in range(24):
        modes = int(rng.integers(3, 9))
        coeffs = []
        for m in range(1, modes + 1):
            amp = 0.95 / (0.55 + 0.35 * m)
            amps = (amp, -amp)
            coeffs.append(float(rng.choice(amps) * rng.uniform(0.35, 1.0)))
        add_start_list(starts, sine_anti(coeffs, n), n)

    # Random anti-complementary block starts.
    for _ in range(16):
        add_start_list(starts, random_block_anti(n, rng), n)

    # Non-anti smooth starts.
    for _ in range(8):
        add_start_list(starts, make_harmonic(n, rng), n)
        add_start_list(starts, smooth_perturb(np.ones(n), rng, rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8)), n)

    # Parent-derived starts.
    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0:
            p = make_safe(p)
            pn = upsample_sequence(p, n)
            add_start_list(starts, pn, n)
            add_start_list(starts, antisym_sequence(pn, 2.0), n)
            add_start_list(starts, smooth_perturb(pn, rng, 0.25), n)
            add_start_list(starts, smooth_perturb(antisym_sequence(pn, 2.0), rng, 0.35), n)

    if not starts:
        starts.append((2.0, normalize_mean(np.ones(n), n)))

    starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
    return starts


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fourier anti-coefficient global search (small dimensional)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _fourier_obj(x):
    n = _FOURIER_N
    bases = _FOURIER_BASES
    if n is None or bases is None or not bases:
        return 1e12
    val = np.full(n, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    for b, c in zip(bases, x):
        val += float(c) * b
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(val)):
        return 1e12
    mn = float(np.min(val))
    mx = float(np.max(val))
    pen = 0.0
    if mn < 0.0:
        pen += 100.0 * (mn * mn)
    if mx > 5.0:
        pen += 10.0 * ((mx - 5.0) * (mx - 5.0))
    a = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
    a = normalize_mean(a, n)
    sc = fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e12 + pen
    return float(sc) + float(pen)


def fourier_anti_search(n, budget_s, seed):
    global _FOURIER_N, _FOURIER_BASES
    n = int(n)
    if n < 16 or budget_s < 30:
        return None, float("inf")

    _FOURIER_N = n
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    modes = 6
    bases = []
    for m in range(1, modes + 1):
        bases.append(np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * m * t))
    _FOURIER_BASES = bases

    amps = (0.95, 0.70, 0.50, 0.35, 0.25, 0.15)
    bounds = [(-amps[i], amps[i]) for i in range(modes)]

    maxiter = int(max(20, min(70, budget_s / 4.0)))
    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.differential_evolution(
            _fourier_obj,
            bounds,
            seed=int(seed),
            maxiter=maxiter,
            popsize=12,
            tol=1e-5,
            polish=False,
            workers=1
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, float("inf")

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    val = np.full(n, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    for b, c in zip(bases, res.x):
        val += float(c) * b

    mn = float(np.min(val))
    if mn < -1e-5:
        val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
        val = antisym_sequence(val, 2.0)

    a = normalize_mean(val, n)
    sc = fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None, float("inf")
    return a, float(sc)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Multiplicative smooth active-set local search
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def weights_from_d(d, beta, K):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = d.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if K and K > 0:
        K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))
        if K >= L:
            return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        idx = np.argpartition(d, -K)[-K:]
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
        return w
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
        return w
    m = float(np.max(d))
    w = np.exp(beta * (d - m))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def local_search(a, budget_s, seed=0, beta_start=3.0, beta_end=80.0,
                 topk_values=(2, 4, 8, 1, 2, 4)):
    a = make_safe(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    target = float(n)
    a = normalize_mean(a, target)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    C = convolve_self(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    score = fast_score_from_conv(a, C)
    L = float(2 * n - 1)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    eta = 0.35
    max_eta = 1.25
    min_eta = 0.005
    noise = 0.015
    it = 0
    it_last_best = 0
    noacc = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.2:
        frac = 0.0
        if budget_s > 1.0:
            frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, (time.monotonic() - t0) / float(budget_s)))

        if frac < 0.55:
            b0 = beta_start
            b1 = max(12.0, 0.25 * beta_end)
            beta = b0 + (b1 - b0) * (frac / 0.55)
            K = 0
        elif frac < 0.85:
            b0 = max(12.0, 0.25 * beta_end)
            b1 = beta_end
            beta = b0 + (b1 - b0) * ((frac - 0.55) / 0.30)
            K = 0
        else:
            beta = 0.0
            K = int(topk_values[it % len(topk_values)])

        S = float(a.sum())
        if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
            a = best.copy()
            C = convolve_self(a)
            S = float(a.sum())
            score = best_score

        d = C * (L / (S * S))
        w = weights_from_d(d, beta, K)
        H = weighted_cross(a, w)
        g = H - float(np.mean(H))
        sc = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0

        if (not np.isfinite(sc)) or sc < 1e-14:
            if it - it_last_best > 250:
                pert = best.copy() * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n))
                pert = normalize_mean(pert, target)
                if fast_score(pert) > best_score * 1.25:
                    pert = best.copy()
                a = pert
                C = convolve_self(a)
                S = float(a.sum())
                score = fast_score_from_conv(a, C)
                eta = 0.55
                noise = 0.05
                noacc = 0
            else:
                break
        else:
            g /= sc
            if noise > 1e-8:
                g += rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
            logf = -eta * g
            logf = np.clip(logf, -2.0, 2.0)
            cand = a * np.exp(logf)
            cand = normalize_mean(cand, target)
            C2 = convolve_self(cand)
            S2 = float(cand.sum())
            cand_score = fast_score_from_conv(cand, C2)

            if np.isfinite(cand_score):
                if cand_score < best_score:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = cand_score
                    it_last_best = it

                if cand_score <= score + 1e-12:
                    old = score
                    a = cand
                    C = C2
                    S = S2
                    score = cand_score
                    noacc = 0
                    if score < old * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                        eta = min(max_eta, eta * 1.08)
                        noise = max(0.002, noise * 0.99)
                    else:
                        eta = max(min_eta, eta * 0.95)
                else:
                    eta = max(min_eta, eta * 0.7)
                    noacc += 1
            else:
                eta = max(min_eta, eta * 0.7)
                noacc += 1

            if noacc > 80:
                eta = 0.6
                noise = 0.08
                noacc = 0

            if it - it_last_best > 600:
                gain = 0.25 if it - it_last_best < 1500 else 0.55
                pert = best.copy() * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, gain, n))
                pert = normalize_mean(pert, target)
                if fast_score(pert) > best_score * 1.25:
                    pert = best.copy()
                a = pert
                C = convolve_self(a)
                S = float(a.sum())
                score = fast_score_from_conv(a, C)
                eta = 0.6
                noise = 0.05
                noacc = 0

        it += 1

    return best, float(best_score)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full L-BFGS refinement in log/softmax space
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _lsz_objective(z):
    global _LSZ_CALLS
    _LSZ_CALLS += 1

    n = int(_LSZ_N)
    if n <= 1:
        return 1e9, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    full[:-1] = z[:n - 1]
    full[-1] = 0.0

    mz = float(np.max(full))
    full = full - mz
    full = np.clip(full, -50.0, 50.0)
    ex = np.exp(full)
    a = ex / float(ex.sum())

    C = convolve_self(a)
    L = float(2 * n - 1)
    d = C * L
    m = float(np.max(d))
    beta = float(_LSZ_BETA)

    if beta <= 1e-8:
        wsum = 1.0
        wh = np.zeros_like(d)
        wh[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
        val = m
    else:
        w = np.exp(beta * (d - m))
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
            wh = np.zeros_like(d)
            wh[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
            val = m
        else:
            wh = w / wsum
            val = m + math.log(wsum) / beta

    H = weighted_cross(a, wh)
    G = 2.0 * H
    b = float(np.dot(a, G))
    grad = L * a * (G - b)

    return float(val), grad


def _lsz_callback(xk):
    if time.monotonic() >= _LSZ_DEADLINE:
        return True
    if _LSZ_CALLS >= _LSZ_MAXFUN:
        return True
    return False


def z_to_a(z, n, target):
    n = int(n)
    full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    full[:-1] = z[:n - 1]
    full[-1] = 0.0
    m = float(np.max(full))
    full = np.clip(full - m, -50.0, 50.0)
    ex = np.exp(full)
    a = ex / float(ex.sum())
    return normalize_mean(a * target, target)


def refine_lbfgs(a, budget_s, beta_values=(10.0, 40.0, 150.0), seed=0):
    global _LSZ_N, _LSZ_BETA, _LSZ_DEADLINE, _LSZ_CALLS, _LSZ_MAXFUN

    a = make_safe(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 5.0:
        return a, fast_score(a)

    tgt = float(n)
    a = normalize_mean(a, tgt)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    for beta in beta_values:
        if time.monotonic() >= end - 1.0:
            break
        _LSZ_N = n
        _LSZ_BETA = float(beta)
        _LSZ_DEADLINE = end
        _LSZ_CALLS = 0
        _LSZ_MAXFUN = 260

        zfull = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
        zfull -= zfull[-1]
        zfull = np.clip(zfull, -35.0, 35.0)
        z0 = zfull[:-1].copy()

        options = {
            "maxiter": 90,
            "maxfun": 260,
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-7,
            "maxls": 20,
            "maxcor": 20,
            "disp": False
        }
        try:
            res = scipy.optimize.minimize(
                _lsz_objective,
                z0,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=options,
                callback=_lsz_callback
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is None:
            continue
        a2 = z_to_a(x, n, tgt)
        sc = fast_score(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a2
            best_score = sc

    return best, float(best_score)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Anti-complementary L-BFGS refinement
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _anti_objective(v):
    global _ANTI_CALLS
    _ANTI_CALLS += 1

    n = int(_ANTI_N)
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 4:
        return 1e9, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = n // 2

    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size != m:
        vv = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        vv[:min(m, v.size)] = v[:min(m, v.size)]
        v = vv

    u = 2.0 * _stable_sigmoid(v)
    u = np.clip(u, 1e-8, 2.0 - 1e-8)

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = u
    a[m:] = 2.0 - u[::-1]

    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return 1e9, np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    C = convolve_self(a)
    L = float(2 * n - 1)
    d = C * (L / (S * S))
    md = float(np.max(d))
    beta = float(_ANTI_BETA)

    if beta <= 1e-8:
        wsum = 1.0
        wh = np.zeros_like(d)
        wh[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
        val = md
    else:
        w = np.exp(beta * (d - md))
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
            wh = np.zeros_like(d)
            wh[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
            val = md
        else:
            wh = w / wsum
            val = md + math.log(wsum) / beta

    H = weighted_cross(a, wh)
    idx = np.arange(m)
    Hdiff = H[idx] - H[n - 1 - idx]
    delta = u * (1.0 - 0.5 * u)
    grad = (L / (S * S)) * (2.0 * delta * Hdiff)

    return float(val), grad


def _anti_callback(xk):
    if time.monotonic() >= _ANTI_DEADLINE:
        return True
    if _ANTI_CALLS >= _ANTI_MAXFUN:
        return True
    return False


def v_to_anti(v, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 4:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = n // 2
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()[:m]
    u = 2.0 * _stable_sigmoid(v)
    u = np.clip(u, 1e-8, 2.0 - 1e-8)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = u
    a[m:] = 2.0 - u[::-1]
    return normalize_mean(a, float(n))


def refine_anti_lbfgs(a, budget_s, beta_values=(10.0, 40.0, 150.0), seed=0):
    global _ANTI_N, _ANTI_BETA, _ANTI_DEADLINE, _ANTI_CALLS, _ANTI_MAXFUN

    a = make_safe(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 4 or n % 2 != 0 or budget_s <= 5.0:
        return a, fast_score(a)

    tgt = float(n)
    a0 = antisym_sequence(a, 2.0)
    a0 = normalize_mean(a0, tgt)

    best = a0.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a0)
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    m = n // 2
    u = a0[:m]
    u = np.clip(u, 1e-7, 2.0 - 1e-7)
    v0 = np.log(u / (2.0 - u))
    v0 = np.clip(v0, -25.0, 25.0)

    for beta in beta_values:
        if time.monotonic() >= end - 1.0:
            break
        _ANTI_N = n
        _ANTI_BETA = float(beta)
        _ANTI_DEADLINE = end
        _ANTI_CALLS = 0
        _ANTI_MAXFUN = 320

        options = {
            "maxiter": 120,
            "maxfun": 320,
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-7,
            "maxls": 20,
            "maxcor": 25,
            "disp": False
        }
        try:
            res = scipy.optimize.minimize(
                _anti_objective,
                v0,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=options,
                callback=_anti_callback
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is None:
            continue
        a2 = v_to_anti(x, n)
        sc = fast_score(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a2
            best_score = sc
            v0 = np.log(np.clip(best[:m], 1e-7, 2.0 - 1e-7) /
                        (2.0 - np.clip(best[:m], 1e-7, 2.0 - 1e-7)))
            v0 = np.clip(v0, -25.0, 25.0)

    return best, float(best_score)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Length pipeline
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def optimize_length(n, budget_s, parent, seed, out_pool):
    n = int(n)
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 5.0:
        seq = normalize_mean(np.ones(n), n)
        return seq, fast_score(seq)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    local_pool = []
    starts = generate_starts(n, parent, rng)

    # Small-dimensional Fourier anti optimal search.
    if budget_s > 600.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 200.0:
        fb = min(220.0, 0.15 * budget_s)
        seq, sc = fourier_anti_search(n, fb, int(seed) + 12345)
        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            add_start_list(local_pool, seq, n)
            add_candidate(local_pool, seq, sc)
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
                quick = min(80.0, 0.35 * fb, deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0)
                seq2, sc2 = local_search(seq, quick, int(seed) + 54321, beta_start=2.0, beta_end=40.0)
                add_candidate(local_pool, seq2, sc2)

    starts.sort(key=_pair_score)

    # Quick refinement of diverse starts.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        qt = min(260.0, 0.18 * budget_s)
        k = min(10, len(starts))
        if k > 0 and qt > 10.0:
            per = max(3.0, qt / float(k))
            for i in range(k):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
                    break
                b = min(per, deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0)
                if b <= 2.0:
                    break
                seq, sc = local_search(
                    starts[i][1],
                    b,
                    int(seed) + 100 + i,
                    beta_start=2.0,
                    beta_end=35.0,
                    topk_values=(2, 4, 8, 16, 2, 4)
                )
                add_candidate(local_pool, seq, sc)

    # Long refinement of the best candidates.
    local_pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if local_pool:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        # Reserve for anti/full L-BFGS and final cleanup.
        reserve = min(420.0, 0.38 * rem)
        lt = max(0.0, rem - reserve - 20.0)
        cand = local_pool[:6]
        if cand and lt > 40.0:
            weights = (5.0, 4.0, 3.0, 2.5, 2.0, 1.5)
            wt = float(sum(weights[:len(cand)]))
            for i, (_, seq) in enumerate(cand):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 40.0:
                    break
                b = lt * (weights[i] / wt)
                b = max(8.0, min(b, deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0))
                if b <= 3.0:
                    break
                seq2, sc2 = local_search(
                    seq,
                    b,
                    int(seed) + 777 + i,
                    beta_start=2.0,
                    beta_end=70.0,
                    topk_values=(2, 4, 1, 4, 8, 2)
                )
                add_candidate(local_pool, seq2, sc2)

    # Anti-manifold L-BFGS.
    local_pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if local_pool:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        ab = min(240.0, 0.25 * rem)
        if ab > 60.0:
            for i in range(min(3, len(local_pool))):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
                    break
                b = ab / 3.0
                b = max(10.0, min(b, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
                if b <= 5.0:
                    break
                try:
                    seq2, sc2 = refine_anti_lbfgs(
                        local_pool[i][1],
                        b,
                        beta_values=(8.0, 30.0, 120.0),
                        seed=int(seed) + 2000 + i
                    )
                    add_candidate(local_pool, seq2, sc2)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Full-space L-BFGS.
    local_pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if local_pool:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        fb = min(200.0, 0.20 * rem)
        if fb > 60.0:
            for i in range(min(2, len(local_pool))):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
                    break
                b = fb / 2.0
                b = max(10.0, min(b, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
                if b <= 5.0:
                    break
                try:
                    seq2, sc2 = refine_lbfgs(
                        local_pool[i][1],
                        b,
                        beta_values=(8.0, 30.0, 120.0),
                        seed=int(seed) + 3000 + i
                    )
                    add_candidate(local_pool, seq2, sc2)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Final long local refinement from current best.
    local_pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if local_pool:
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0)
        if rem > 40.0:
            try:
                best_seq, best_sc = local_pool[0]
                seq2, sc2 = local_search(
                    best_seq,
                    rem,
                    int(seed) + 999999,
                    beta_start=3.0,
                    beta_end=100.0,
                    topk_values=(2, 4, 1, 4, 8, 2)
                )
                add_candidate(local_pool, seq2, sc2)
            except Exception:
                pass

    local_pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if local_pool:
        best_seq, best_sc = local_pool[0]
    else:
        best_seq = normalize_mean(np.ones(n), n)
        best_sc = fast_score(best_seq)

    for item in local_pool:
        add_candidate(out_pool, item[1], item[0], max_size=80)

    return best_seq, float(best_sc)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Final sanitization and exact validation helper
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if seq.size == 0:
        return [1.0] * 512

    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 512

    # Normalize to unit mean if this does not violate the 1000 cap.
    scale = float(seq.size) / s
    if float(seq.max()) * scale <= 1000.0:
        seq = seq * scale
    else:
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(seq.sum())
        if s > 0.01:
            scale = float(seq.size) / s
            if float(seq.max()) * scale <= 1000.0:
                seq = seq * scale

    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 512
    return [float(x) for x in seq]


def safe_exact_eval(seq):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(sanitize_for_return(seq)))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    if budget < 30.0:
        return [1.0] * 512

    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    global_pool = []

    print(f"[propose] total budget = {budget:.1f}s")

    # Choose base length. The known construction length is 512; we primarily
    # optimize that length and optionally extend if time remains.
    base_n = 512

    if budget > 1500.0:
        base_budget = max(600.0, budget - 800.0)
    elif budget > 700.0:
        base_budget = max(300.0, budget - 220.0)
    else:
        base_budget = max(20.0, budget - 20.0)

    print(f"[propose] base length = {base_n}, base budget = {base_budget:.1f}s")
    try:
        base_seq, base_fast = optimize_length(base_n, base_budget, parent, int(seed), global_pool)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[propose] base optimize failed: {e}")
        base_seq = normalize_mean(np.ones(base_n), base_n)
        base_fast = fast_score(base_seq)

    base_exact = safe_exact_eval(base_seq)
    print(f"[propose] base fast = {base_fast:.9f}, exact = {base_exact:.9f}")

    best_seq = base_seq.copy() if base_seq is not None else normalize_mean(np.ones(base_n), base_n)
    best_exact = base_exact

    # Optional longer length refinement if time remains and base did not already exceed target.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    remaining = budget - elapsed
    if remaining > 700.0 and best_exact > 1.5027:
        long_n = 1024
        long_budget = min(650.0, max(120.0, remaining - 180.0))
        print(f"[propose] trying longer length = {long_n}, budget = {long_budget:.1f}s")
        try:
            long_seq, long_fast = optimize_length(long_n, long_budget, best_seq, int(seed) + 100000, global_pool)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[propose] long optimize failed: {e}")
            long_seq, long_fast = None, float("inf")

        if long_seq is not None:
            long_exact = safe_exact_eval(long_seq)
            print(f"[propose] longer fast = {long_fast:.9f}, exact = {long_exact:.9f}")
            if np.isfinite(long_exact) and long_exact < best_exact:
                best_seq = long_seq.copy()
                best_exact = long_exact
            add_candidate(global_pool, long_seq, long_exact, max_size=80)

    # Final exact validation across the pool.
    global_pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    # Include the current best explicitly.
    add_candidate(global_pool, best_seq, best_exact, max_size=80)
    global_pool.sort(key=_pair_score)

    top_n = min(12, len(global_pool))
    for i in range(top_n):
        _, seq = global_pool[i]
        sc = safe_exact_eval(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_seq = seq
            print(f"[propose] exact improvement {i}: {best_exact:.9f}")

    if not np.isfinite(best_exact) or best_seq is None:
        best_seq = normalize_mean(np.ones(base_n), base_n)
        best_exact = 2.0

    out = sanitize_for_return(best_seq)
    final_exact = safe_exact_eval(out)
    print(f"[propose] final length = {len(out)}, final exact = {final_exact:.9f}")
    return out
```
